# Bitvavo paper setups by coin

All times are Vienna time in November 2026. The ledger covers the windows in the [archive index](README.md). Earlier confirmations appear only when they link to an in-range follow-up. For duplicate events, the ledger prefers hourly output, then manual output, then formatted reports.

A follow-up is linked only when exactly one available confirmation for that market has the same reference price and falls within the two-hour retest window. A retest expiry links to the confirmation exactly two hours earlier. Paper outcomes use the logged reference and retest time. Unlinked observations retain their details without guessing the signal. A dash means the source provides no value or no linked event. The ±10% levels are calculated from the paper reference, and a retest records a trade at or below that reference.
